fun E [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] => { F // IsClosed ↑F ∧ FiniteDimensional ℝ (E ⧸ F) }
def Chal.ForestSpace.aucValues : (E : Type) →
  [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E] → [inst_1 : NormedSpace ℝ E] → (E → ℝ) → ℝ → E → Chal.ForestSpace.Cofinite E → Set ℝ :=
fun E [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] N t x F => {a | ∃ y ∈ ↑F, N y = 1 ∧ a = N (x + t • y) - 1}

def Chal.ForestSpace.averageValues : (E : Type) →
  [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E] → [inst_1 : NormedSpace ℝ E] → (E → ℝ) → ℝ → E → Chal.ForestSpace.Cofinite E → Set ℝ :=
fun E [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] N t x F =>
  {a | ∃ y ∈ ↑F, N y = 1 ∧ a = (N (x + t • y) + N (x - t • y)) / ↑2 - 1}

def Chal.ForestSpace.IsSegment : {V : Type} → [PartialOrder V] → Finset V → Prop :=
fun {V} [PartialOrder V] S => (∀ a ∈ S, ∀ b ∈ S, a ≤ b ∨ b ≤ a) ∧ ∀ a ∈ S, ∀ b ∈ S, ∀ (c : V), a ≤ c → c ≤ b → c ∈ S

fun A =>
  { Adj := fun u v => ∃ e, A.left e = u ∧ A.right e = v ∨ A.right e = u ∧ A.left e = v, symm := ⋯, loopless := ⋯ }

  Chal.ForestSpace.Diamond.Network :=

def Chal.ForestSpace.Diamond.EmbeddingBound : {E : Type} →
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] → (k : ℕ) → ((Chal.ForestSpace.Diamond.diamond k).V → E) → ℝ → Prop :=
fun {E} [NormedAddCommGroup E] k f C =>
  ∃ s,
    0 < s ∧
      ∀ (u v : (Chal.ForestSpace.Diamond.diamond k).V),
        s * Chal.ForestSpace.Diamond.distance k u v ≤ ‖f u - f v‖ ∧
          ‖f u - f v‖ ≤ C * s * Chal.ForestSpace.Diamond.distance k u v
